Well, back in 2010, a group of guys in Abilene, Texas decided to have a reunion of sorts. A chance to get some of the old bands back together — to see if they could still “bring it”. Needless to say, it was a success. And a dozen years later, it’s still happening once a year - and the crowd continues to grow every year. Usually in the summer, 6/7 bands will come together and play some of the great music from the 60’s, 70’s and 80’s.
